2026-10-23T00:02:46.2610927Z env:

2026-10-23T00:02:46.2611318Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-23T00:02:46.2618913Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:aspire2ap] [build-only] Build the Decode round-2 image: opt-in dense GEMM configs and MegaMoE L2 prefetch in the DeepGEMM host, plus a pre-serving Decode kernel selection

Main 3642c64 (8481.99) is Decode bound. Its Decode step is 31.44 ms per rank: MegaMoE 200.8 us per layer, FA3 verify 51.7 us, dense FP8 QKV/O 31.9 us (17.3 + 14.3), and a cuBLAS split-K router gate GEMM 6.9 us. This commit carries three candidates. Each one is off unless a Decode env enables it. The user wants as few Firmus submissions as possible, so there is no separate kernel bench. The serving job measures the candidates on four H200s before model startup, keeps only clear wins, and archives every measurement.

Host (patches/deep-gemm-host/series, rebuilt into _C.so by this build):
- sm90-1d2d-decode-config.patch replaces the rejected sm90-1d2d-small-m.patch (B5 picked BLOCK_M 256 and was 2.6x slower).
  - DG_SM90_1D2D_DECODE_CONFIG="QKV:bm,bn,stages,mc,mcA;O:..." pins the 1d2d config of the two Decode projection shapes (N9216/K4096 and N4096/K8192) for M <= 128.
  - Unset, other shapes, and M > 128 keep the stock heuristic; a native CPU check covers 88 cases.
  - Candidates: stock tile with fewer stages, TMA multicast on A or B, BLOCK_M 128 with BLOCK_N 80/32/64. All 18 configs compile with 0 B spill.
- sm90-decode-l2-prefetch.patch: DG_SM90_DECODE_L2_PREFETCH_STAGES (0/8/16) becomes a template argument of the Decode SwapAB MegaMoE instantiation. 0 or unset keeps the old instantiation string and JIT cache key.

DeepGEMM header overlay:
- sm90-decode-swapab-l2-prefetch.patch: during the 17-20 us token dispatch wait, the B loader of each CTA's first FC1 block prefetches the next 8 or 16 weight stages into L2. This is 17-35 MB per rank, using cp.async.bulk.prefetch.tensor through the existing tensor map.
  - No barrier, arrival count or phase changes.
  - With 0, the SASS of Decode, Prefill 2WG, Prefill 512-thread and non-SwapAB Decode matches control.
  - Target 3-6 us per layer; unmeasured.

SGLang overlays (Decode only; unset means the current paths):
- decode-dense-gemm.patch: SGLANG_DECODE_DENSE_GEMM_QKV / _O switch the two Decode projections to the image's Triton or FlashInfer SM90 block-scale FP8 GEMM. Both use per-token per-128 activation scales, 128x128 weight scales and FP32 accumulation.
- decode-gate-gemm.patch: SGLANG_DECODE_GATE_GEMM=triton replaces the cuBLAS split-K BF16 router gate GEMM (4096 -> 128) with one Triton kernel. It accumulates the full K in FP32 and stores BF16 once. Target -0.27 to -0.36 ms per step.

Pre-serving selection (scripts/select-firmus-decode-kernels.sh, run on the head node's four GPUs before the numeric gate):
- select-firmus-decode-dense.py:
  - Checks every dense candidate numerically: FP32 oracle, stock, and changed-input CUDA graph replays.
  - Times the candidates round-robin against stock in two blocks.
  - Per projection, picks the fastest candidate that beats stock by >= 0.5 us in the median and in both blocks.
- bench-firmus-sglang-kernels.py --select-decode-gate: runs the gate numeric checks, then cuBLAS vs Triton ABAB on 94 layers of weights. Needs >= 200 us per step on every GPU and in both blocks.
- bench-firmus-megamoe.py --tune-l2-prefetch:
  - Cold-L2 replays with a 128 MiB read inside each graph; only the MegaMoE interval is timed, ABC order over 0/8/16.
  - Needs >= 2.13 us in every case and both blocks.
  - No Decode expert-count dump exists, so the measured Prefill frequencies (run 39051152456, REAL_ROUTING_LAYERS) stand in. The routing file labels them phase="prefill-proxy".
- The selected env lines go to source/decode-kernels.env, which only the Decode worker sources. The numeric gate then runs with the same env and --dense-selected, so the chosen kernels are checked again.
- A probe that fails or times out selects nothing. Its GPU code has never run, and one probe failure should not cost a serving allocation that queues for hours.

Not done: sharding the EAGLE draft weights across ranks. The draft is fully replicated on each DP rank, but its GEMMs are 0.89 ms per step and the communication makes the gain uncertain. The draft lm_head vocab split stays a later candidate.

Tests: the full qwen-experiment-metrics suite passes locally. The worker-launch fixtures now provide the empty decode-kernels.env, and the gate-entrypoint fixture mocks torch.cuda.set_device. The host series applies on DeepGEMM v0.1.5 in order: swap-ab-144, prefill-2wg, 1d2d-decode-config, decode-l2-prefetch. After this build, a serving commit pins the new image.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
